<script>
	function hideLoader(){
		var checkForLoader = setInterval(function(){
			var element =  document.getElementById('loading-state');
			if (typeof(element) != 'undefined' && element != null){
				element.classList.add('hidden');
				clearInterval(checkForLoader);
			}
		}, 200);
	}
	(function( w ){
		"use strict";
		// rel=preload support test
		if( !w.loadCSS ){
			w.loadCSS = function(){};
		}
		// define on the loadCSS obj
		var rp = loadCSS.relpreload = {};
		// rel=preload feature support test
		// runs once and returns a function for compat purposes
		rp.support = (function(){
			var ret;
			try {
				ret = w.document.createElement( "link" ).relList.supports( "preload" );
			} catch (e) {
				ret = false;
			}
			return function(){
				return ret;
			};
		})();

		// if preload isn't supported, get an asynchronous load by using a non-matching media attribute
		// then change that media back to its intended value on load
		rp.bindMediaToggle = function( link ){
			// remember existing media attr for ultimate state, or default to 'all'
			var finalMedia = link.media || "all";

			function enableStylesheet(){
				link.media = finalMedia;
			}

			// bind load handlers to enable media
			if( link.addEventListener ){
				link.addEventListener( "load", enableStylesheet );
			} else if( link.attachEvent ){
				link.attachEvent( "onload", enableStylesheet );
			}

			// Set rel and non-applicable media type to start an async request
			// note: timeout allows this to happen async to let rendering continue in IE
			setTimeout(function(){
				link.rel = "stylesheet";
				link.media = "only x";
			});
			// also enable media after 3 seconds,
			// which will catch very old browsers (android 2.x, old firefox) that don't support onload on link
			setTimeout( enableStylesheet, 300 );
		};

		// loop through link elements in DOM
		rp.poly = function(){
			// double check this to prevent external calls from running
			if( rp.support() ){
				return;
			}
			var links = w.document.getElementsByTagName( "link" );
			for( var i = 0; i < links.length; i++ ){
				var link = links[ i ];
				// qualify links to those with rel=preload and as=style attrs
				if( link.rel === "preload" && link.getAttribute( "as" ) === "style" && !link.getAttribute( "data-loadcss" ) ){
					// prevent rerunning on link
					link.setAttribute( "data-loadcss", true );
					// bind listeners to toggle media back
					rp.bindMediaToggle( link );
				}
			}
		};

		// if unsupported, run the polyfill
		if( !rp.support() ){
			// run once at least
			rp.poly();

			// rerun poly on an interval until onload
			var run = w.setInterval( rp.poly, 500 );
			if( w.addEventListener ){
				w.addEventListener( "load", function(){
					rp.poly();
					w.clearInterval( run );
				} );
			} else if( w.attachEvent ){
				w.attachEvent( "onload", function(){
					rp.poly();
					w.clearInterval( run );
				} );
			}
		}


		// commonjs
		if( typeof exports !== "undefined" ){
			exports.loadCSS = loadCSS;
		}
		else {
			w.loadCSS = loadCSS;
		}
	}( typeof global !== "undefined" ? global : this ) );

<section class="pos-fixed height-100 width-100 hidden zIndex-1000 overlay-tint-error tp-0" id="blockedNotifPrompt">
<section class="wrapper">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 col-xl-4">
<div class="bg-white border-box notification-prompt">
<div class="dp-flex fx-justify-end">
<p class="nb-icon-cancel text-light fz-12 cs-pointer cancel errorClose"></p>
</div>
<div class="dp-flex fx-dr-col fx-align-center mg-t-21 text-black ff-montserrat fw-4 fz-lg-14 lh-lg-18 ls-1">
<img alt="How to enable Notifications" class="lazy notif-block-img" data-original="https://o.cdn.newsbytesapp.com/assets/images/webNotifs/notify.png" src="data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAABAAAAAJCAYAAAA7KqwyAAAABGdBTUEAALGPC/xhBQAAAB1JREFUKBVjfPn2438GCgATBXrBWkcNYGAYBmEAAK02A9g3RmLgAAAAAElFTkSuQmCC"/>
<p class="text-black ff-montserrat fw-7 fz-lg-14 lh-lg-18 ls-1 mg-t-9">Enable Browser Push Notification</p>
<div class="mg-t-21">
<span class="allowNotifStep fz-15 text-green dp-ib text-center">1</span>
</div>
<div class="text-center">
<span class="chrome hidden">Click on the lock icon right next to website address in your browser.</span>
<span class="firefox hidden">Click on the lock icon right next to website address in your browser.</span>
<span class="hidden safari">Open preferences by clicking safari icon in toolbar.</span>
</div>
<div class="mg-t-6 text-center text-green fz-20">|</div>
<div class="mg-t-6">
<span class="allowNotifStep fz-15 text-green dp-ib text-center">2</span>
</div>
<div class="text-center">
<span class="chrome hidden">Notification permissions selection for the website are displayed in a dropdown, click on Site settings</span>
<span class="firefox hidden">Notification permissions selection for the website are displayed in a dropdown, click on Site settings</span>
<span class="hidden safari">Open 'Notifications' tab, you will see all the existing notifications.</span>
</div>
<div class="mg-t-6 text-center text-green fz-20">|</div>
<div class="mg-t-6">
<span class="allowNotifStep fz-15 text-green dp-ib text-center">3</span>
</div>
<div class="text-center">
<span class="chrome hidden">Choose Allow from 'Notifications' permission to enable notifications.</span>
<span class="firefox hidden">Choose Allow from 'Notifications' permission to enable notifications.</span>
<span class="hidden safari">Select Allow to enable notifications.</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<script src="/js/story.50e9a0.js" type="text/javascript"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){
                var storiesData = {};
                var currentStory = "";
                var slideCount = 0;
                var totalSlidesInStory = 0;
                var storiesIdColl = [];
                var loadedNewData = false;
                
                $.storage_available = function(type){
                    var storage;
                    try {
                        storage = window[type];
                        var x = '__storage_test__';
                        storage.setItem(x, x);
                        storage.removeItem(x);
                        return true;
                    }
                    catch(e) {
                        return e instanceof DOMException && (
                            // everything except Firefox
                            e.code === 22 ||
                            // Firefox
                            e.code === 1014 ||
                            // test name field too, because code might not be present
                            // everything except Firefox
                            e.name === 'QuotaExceededError' ||
                            // Firefox
                            e.name === 'NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED') &&
                            // acknowledge QuotaExceededError only if there's something already stored
                            (storage && storage.length !== 0);
                    }
                }

                $.story_item_render = function(item, html){
                    totalSlidesInStory = storiesData[currentStory]['combined'].length;
                    var data = storiesData[currentStory]['combined'][slideCount];
                    var templateType = data['template'];
                    var clonedBlock = $(".storySlide" + "[data-template=" + '"' + templateType + '"' + "]").clone();
                    clonedBlock.find('.story-slide').removeClass('hidden');
                    clonedBlock.find('.title').text(data['name']);

                    switch(templateType){
                        case "image":
                            clonedBlock.find('.category-tag').text(storiesData[currentStory]['info']['category']).addClass('bk-' + storiesData[currentStory]['info']['category'].toLowerCase());
                            clonedBlock.find('.sub-title').text(data['visibletimestamp']);
                            if(slideCount < 1){clonedBlock.find('.category-tag').removeClass('hidden')}
                        break;
                        case "share":
                            clonedBlock.find('.tl-url').attr("href", storiesData[currentStory]['info']['timelineUrl']);
                            clonedBlock.find('.next-story-section *').removeClass('hidden');
                            clonedBlock.find('.share-story').each(function(item){
                                var url = $(this).data('template').replace(/<url>/g, storiesData[currentStory]['info']['timelineUrl']);
                                $(this).attr('href', url);
                            });
                        break;
                        case "video":
                            clonedBlock.find('.video-frame').attr('src', data['video']);
                            clonedBlock.find('.sub-title').text(data['visibletimestamp']);
                            html = ""; 
                    }
                    slideCount++;   
                    html = html + clonedBlock.html();
                    return html;
                }

                $.story_carousel = function(response, timelineId){
                    //Code for the transformation
                    $('style[data-style=story]').remove();
                    var storyPreview = [];
                    storiesIdColl = [];
                    var backgroundImageCss = "";

                    if(response.length > 0){
                        response.forEach(function(item){
                            var id = item['info']['id'];
                            storiesData[id] = item;
                            var story =  {
                                id: item['info']['id'],
                                photo: item['info']['photo'],
                                name: item['info']['name'],
                                link: "",
                                lastUpdated: "",
                                items: []     
                            }
                            backgroundImageCss = backgroundImageCss + ' .stories.carousel .story[data-id=' + '"' + item['info']['id'] + '"' + ']:before{ background-image: url(' + item['info']['photo'] + ');}';
                            var slideLength = item.combined.length;
                            item.combined.forEach(function(slide, i, arr){
                                switch (slide['type']){
                                    case "photo":
                                    slide.template = "image";
                                    break;
                                    case "video":
                                    slide.template = "video";
                                    break;
                                }
                                var slideItem = {
                                    id: slide['id'],
                                    type: slide['type'],
                                    length: slide['length'],
                                    src: slide['image'] || slide['video'],
                                    preview: "",
                                    link: "",
                                    linkText: "",
                                    time: "",
                                    seen: false
                                }
                                story.items.push(slideItem);
                            });
                            var shareSlideData = {
                                timelineId: item['info']['id'],
                                id: Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 5),
                                visibletimestamp: "",
                                video: "",
                                image: item['info']['photo'],
                                name: item['info']['name'],
                                type: "photo",
                                template: "share",
                                length: 60,
                                timelineUrl: item['info']['timelineUrl']
                            }
                            var shareSlideItem = {
                                id: shareSlideData['id'],
                                type: shareSlideData['type'],
                                length: shareSlideData['length'],
                                src: shareSlideData['image'],
                                preview: "",
                                link: "",
                                linkText: "",
                                time: "",
                                seen: false
                            }
                            item.combined.push(shareSlideData);
                            story.items.push(shareSlideItem);
                            storyPreview.push(story);
                        });
                        $('<style type="text/css" data-style="story">' + backgroundImageCss + '</style>').appendTo("body");
                        
                        //Initializing the plugin
                        var carouselType = (window.innerWidth < 768)?'post':'card'; 

                        $("#stories-card *").remove();
                        $("#stories-list *").remove();
                        if(typeof timelineId !== "undefined"){
                            carouselType = carouselType + "-" + timelineId;
                        }
                        if($("#stories-" + carouselType).length > 0){
                            var stories = new Zuck('stories-' + carouselType, {
                                backNative: false,
                                previousTap: true,
                                openEffect: false,
                                autoFullScreen: true,
                                skin: 'snapgram',
                                avatars: false,
                                list: false,
                                cubeEffect: false,
                                localStorage: true,
                                stories: storyPreview,
                                updateStoryseenPosition: false,
                                callbacks:  {
                                    'onOpen': function(storyId, callback) {
                                        slideCount = 0;
                                        totalSlidesInStory = 0;
                                        callback();
                                    },
                                    
                                    'fetchStoryId': function(storyId){
                                        currentStory = storyId.toString();
                                        slideCount = 0;
                                        totalSlidesInStory = 0;
                                    },
                                    
                                    'onRender': function(item, mediaHTML) {
                                        mediaHTML = $.story_item_render(item, mediaHTML);
                                        return mediaHTML;
                                    },
                                    
                                    'onView': function(storyId) {
                                        if(storyId == storiesIdColl[storiesIdColl.length - 1]){$('.next-story-section *').addClass('hidden');}
                                    },
                                    
                                    'onEnd': function(storyId, callback) {
                                        callback();  // on end story                                
                                    },
                                    
                                    'onClose': function(storyId, callback) {
                                        callback();  // on close story viewer
                                        
                                    },
                                    
                                    'onNavigateItem': function(storyId, nextStoryId, callback) {
                                        callback();  // on navigate item of story
                                    },
                                },
                            });
                            
                            var storySlider = {
                                infinite: false,
                                slidesToShow: 1,
                                variableWidth: true,
                                nextArrow: '<div class="cs-pointer pos-absolute tp-0 height-100 border-box right-0 dp-flex fx-align-center" style="z-index:5;"><div class="dp-flex fx-align-center fx-justify-center"><p class="nb-icon-angle-down text-light-slate-grey fz-20 bg-white pd-t-18 pd-b-15 pd-l-14 pd-r-14" style="transform:rotate(-90deg);border-top-left-radius: 5px;border-top-right-radius: 5px;"></p></div></div>',
                                prevArrow: '<div class="cs-pointer pos-absolute tp-0 height-100 border-box left-0 dp-flex fx-align-center" style="z-index:5;display:flex !important;"><div class="dp-flex fx-align-center fx-justify-center"><p class="nb-icon-angle-down text-light-slate-grey fz-20 bg-white pd-t-14 pd-l-14 pd-r-14" style="transform:rotate(90deg);border-top-left-radius: 5px;border-top-right-radius: 5px;padding-bottom:8px;margin-left:-1px;"></p></div></div>',
                                responsive: [			
                                    {
                                        breakpoint: 767,
                                        settings: "unslick"
                                    }
                                ]
                            };
                            
                            $('.stories-init-parent').each(function(item){
                                if($(this).data('slick') == 'enabled'){
                                    $(this).find('.stories.carousel').slick(storySlider);
                                }
                            });
                            
                            $('.stories-init-parent .story').each(function(i,item){
                                storiesIdColl[i] = $(this).data('id');
                            });
                            $('.carousel-template-post').removeClass('hidden').addClass('dp-hidden-lg');
                            $('.carousel-template-card').removeClass('hidden').addClass('is-hidden-in-mobile');
                        }
                    }else{
                        return
                    }
                }
                
                $.story_init = function(timelineId){
                    if ($.storage_available('localStorage') && (localStorage.getItem('_stories_data'))) {
                        $.story_carousel(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('_stories_data')), timelineId);
                    }
                }
                
                $.story_load = function(){
                    $.ajaxCall.get("/story/list", {}, function(response, status, statusCode){
                        if(status){
                            loadedNewData = true;
                            if($.storage_available('localStorage')){
                                localStorage.removeItem('_stories_data');
                                localStorage.setItem('_stories_data', response);
                            }
                            $.story_carousel(JSON.parse(response));
                        }else{

                        }
                    });
                }

                $.story_init();

                $(window).scroll(function(){
                    if (($(window).scrollTop() > ($("body").height() - ($("body").height()*0.65))) && (!loadedNewData)) {
                        $.story_load();
                        loadedNewData = true;
                    }else{
                        return
                    }
                });

            });
        </script>
<script type="text/javascript">
        var newWorker;
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){
            pwa = [];
            if(typeof window.PWA != 'undefined')
                pwa = new window.PWA;
            if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
                    var deferredPrompt;

                    function checkBttn(){
                        var element = document.getElementById('installPwa');
                        if (typeof(element) != 'undefined' && element != null) return true;
                        else return false;
                    }
                    
                    navigator.serviceWorker.register('/OneSignalSDKWorker.js',{scope:'/'}).then(
                        function (reg) {
                            console.log('ServiceWorker registration successful with scope: ', reg.scope);
                            if (reg.installing) {
                                newWorker = reg.installing;
                                console.log('Service worker installing');
                            } else if (reg.waiting) {
                                newWorker = reg.waiting;
                                console.log('Service worker installed & waiting');
                            } else if (reg.active) {
                                newWorker = reg.active;
                                console.log('Service worker active');
                                pwa.push(['init']);
                            }

                            reg.onupdatefound = function(){
                                const installingWorker = reg.installing;
                                installingWorker.onstatechange = function(){
                                    switch (installingWorker.state) {
                                        case 'installed':
                                            if (navigator.serviceWorker.controller) {
                                                console.log('show the prompt');
                                                pwa.push(['updateNotif']);
                                                newWorkerInstalled();
                                            } else {
                                                console.log('no update available');
                                            }
                                            break;
                                    }
                                };

                                function newWorkerInstalled(){
                                    setTimeout(function(){
                                        pwa.push(['hideUpdateNotif']);
                                    }, 10000);
                                    $('#reload').click(function(){
                                        reg.update();
                                        pwa.push(['hideUpdateNotif']);
                                        location.reload();
                                    });
                                    $('#dismiss-app-update').click(function(){
                                        pwa.push(['hideUpdateNotif']);
                                    });
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        function (err) {
                            console.error('unsuccessful registration with ', err);
                        }
                    );
                    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', function (e) {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        deferredPrompt = e;
                        if(checkBttn())
                        // document.getElementById('installPwa').classList.remove("hidden");
                        document.getElementById('installPwa').removeAttribute("style");
                    }); 
                    
                    if(checkBttn()){
                        var btnAdd = document.getElementById('installPwa');
                        btnAdd.addEventListener('click', function(e){
                            deferredPrompt.prompt();
                            ga("send", "event", "pwa", "click", window.location.href);
                            deferredPrompt.userChoice
                            .then(function(choiceResult){
                                if (choiceResult.outcome === 'accepted') {
                                    console.log('User accepted the add-to-homescreen prompt');
                                    ga("send", "event", "pwa", "accepted", window.location.href);
                                    btnAdd.parentNode.removeChild(btnAdd);
                                } else {
                                    console.log('User dismissed the add-to-homescreen prompt');
                                    ga("send", "event", "pwa", "rejected", window.location.href);
                                    btnAdd.parentNode.removeChild(btnAdd);
                                }
                                deferredPrompt = null;
                            });
                        });
                    }
                }
        });
        </script>
